## 29. Codex

```text

## 49. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T14:35:40.752Z` · source line `1060`

```text
Notebook C++ bounded selection уже корректно ограничивает память, но я направил ещё одно ускорение: не allgather’ить все ~50 млн hit-records при `2**21×24`. Каждый rank локально оставляет свой next-K после cursor, а NCCL обменивает только ≤K с каждого rank; глобальный K математически всегда лежит в объединении локальных K. Это сохраняет точность и резко режет PCIe/NCCL трафик без нового CUDA kernel.
```

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

Optional argument.\nL192:     * See cite56†QueuesContentType for possible values.\nL193:   * `delaySeconds` `number`\nL194:     * The number of seconds to cite5†delay a message for within the queue, before it can be delivered to a consumer.\nL195:     * Must be an integer between 0 and 86400 (24 hours).\nL196: ### `QueueSendOptions`\nL197: \nL198: Optional configuration that applies when sending a message to a queue.\nL199:   * `contentType` `QueuesContentType`\nL200:     * The explicit content type of a message so it can be previewed correctly with the cite24†List messages from the dashboard feature. Optional argument.\nL201:     * As of now, this option is for internal use. In the future, `contentType` will be used by alternative consumer types to explicitly mark messages as serialized so they can be consumed in the desired type.\nL202:     * See cite56†QueuesContentType for possible values.\nL203:   * `delaySeconds` `number`\nL204:     * The number of seconds to cite5†delay a message for within the queue, before it can be delivered to a consumer.\nL205:     * Must be an integer between 0 and 86400 (24 hours). Setting this value to zero will explicitly prevent the message from being delayed, even if there is a global (default) delay at the queue level.\nL206: ### `QueueSendBatchOptions`\nL207: \nL208: Optional configuration that applies when sending a batch of messages to a queue.\nL209: \nL210:   * `delaySeconds` `number`\nL211:     * The number of seconds to cite5†delay messages for within the queue, before it can be delivered to a consumer.\nL212:     * Must be a positive integer.\nL213: ### `QueuesContentType`\nL214: \nL215: A union type containing valid message content types.\nL216: \nL217:     `// Default: json\nL218:     type QueuesContentType = \"text\" | \"bytes\" | \"json\" | \"v8\";`\nL219:   * Use `\"json\"` to send a JavaScript object that can be JSON-serialized. This content type can be previewed from the cite57†Cloudflare dashboard ↗†dash.cloudflare.com . The `json` content type is the default.\nL220:   * Use `\"text\"` to send a `String`. This content type can be previewed with the cite24†List messages from the dashboard feature.\nL221:   * Use `\"bytes\"` to send an `ArrayBuffer`. This content type cannot be previewed from the cite57†Cloudflare dashboard ↗†dash.cloudflare.com and will display as Base64-encoded.\nL222:   * Use `\"v8\"` to send a JavaScript object that cannot be JSON-serialized but is supported by cite51†structured clone ↗†developer.mozilla.org (for example `Date` and `Map`). This content type cannot be previewed from the cite57†Cloudflare dashboard ↗†dash.cloudflare.com and will display as Base64-encoded.\nL223: Note\nL224: \nL225: The default content type for Queues changed to `json` (from `v8`) to improve compatibility with pull-based consumers for any Workers with a cite58†compatibility date after `2024-03-18`.\nL226: \nL227: If you specify an invalid content type, or if your specified content type does not match the message content's type, the send operation will fail with an error.\nL228: ### `QueueSendResult`\nL229: \nL230: The result of a successful send operation.\nL231: \nL232:     `interface QueueSendResult {\nL233:     \tmetadata: {\nL234:     \t\tmetrics: QueueMetrics;\nL235:     \t};\nL236:     }`\nL237: \nL238:   * `metadata` `object`\nL239:     * Contains metadata about the queue after the send operation.\nL240:   * `metadata.metrics` `QueueMetrics`\nL241:     * Realtime metrics for the queue. See cite55†QueueMetrics .\nL242: ### `QueueMetrics`\nL243: \nL244: Realtime metrics for a queue.\nL245: \nL246:     `interface QueueMetrics {\nL247:     \tbacklogCount: number;\nL248:     \tbacklogBytes: number;\nL249:     \toldestMessageTimestamp: number;\nL250:     }`\nL251: \nL252:   * `backlogCount` `number`\nL253:     * The number of messages currently in the queue.\nL254:   * `backlogBytes` `number`\nL255:     * The total size of messages in the queue, in bytes.\nL256:   * `oldestMessageTimestamp` `number`\nL257:     * The timestamp (in milliseconds since epoch) of the oldest message in the queue.\nL258: ## Consumer\nL259: \nL260: These APIs allow a consumer Worker to consume messages from a Queue.\nL261: \nL262: To define a consumer Worker, add a `queue()` function to the default export of the Worker. This will allow it to receive messages from the Queue.\nL263: \nL264: By default, all messages in the batch will be acknowledged as soon as all of the following conditions are met:\nL265:   1. The `queue()` function has returned.\nL266:   2. If the `queue()` function returned a promise, the promise has resolved.\nL267:   3. Any promises passed to `waitUntil()` have resolved.\nL268: \nL269: If the `queue()` function throws, or the promise returned by it or any of the promises passed to `waitUntil()` were rejected, then the entire batch will be considered a failure and will be retried according to the consumer's retry settings.\nL270: \nL271: Note\nL272: `waitUntil()` is the only supported method to run tasks (such as logging or metrics calls) that resolve after a queue handler has completed. Promises that have not resolved by the time the queue handler returns may not complete and will not block completion of execution.\nL273: \nL274: index.js js\nL275: \nL276:     `export default {\nL277:     \tasync queue(batch, env, ctx) {\nL278:     \t\tfor (const message of batch.messages) {\nL279:     \t\t\tconsole.log(\"Received\", message.body);\nL280:     \t\t}\nL281:     \t},\nL282:     };`\nL283: index.ts ts\nL284: \nL285:     `interface Env {\nL286:       // Add your bindings here\nL287:     }\nL288: \nL289:     export default {\nL290:       async queue(batch, env, ctx): Promise<void> {\nL291:         for (const message of batch.messages) {\nL292:           console.log(\"Received\", message.body);\nL293:         }\nL294:       },\nL295:     } satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;`\nL296: \nL297:     `from workers import WorkerEntrypoint\nL298: \nL299:     class Default(WorkerEntrypoint):\nL300:         async def queue(self, batch):\nL301:             for message in batch.messages:\nL302:                 print(\"Received\", message)`\nL303: The `env` and `ctx` fields are as cite59†documented in the Workers documentation .\nL304: ### TypeScript message types\nL305: \nL306: You can type queue messages with `Queue<T>` on the producer and `ExportedHandler<Env, T>` on the consumer.\nL307: \nL308:     `type MyMessage = {\nL309:       id: string;\nL310:     };\nL311: \nL312:     interface Env {\nL313:       MY_QUEUE: Queue<MyMessage>;\nL314:     }\nL315: \nL316:     export default {\nL317:       async queue(batch) {\nL318:         for (const message of batch.messages) {\nL319:           console.log(message.body.id);\nL320:         }\nL321:       },\nL322:     } satisfies ExportedHandler<Env, MyMessage>;`\nL323: For primitive messages, use `Queue<number>` or `satisfies ExportedHandler<Env, number>`. If you do not specify a type, `message.body` is `unknown`.\nL324: \nL325: Or alternatively, a queue consumer can be written using the (deprecated) service worker syntax:\nL326: \nL327:     `addEventListener('queue', (event) => {\nL328:     \tevent.waitUntil(handleMessages(event));\nL329:     });`\nL330: In service worker syntax, `event` provides the same fields and methods as `MessageBatch`, as defined below, in addition to cite60†`waitUntil()` ↗†developer.mozilla.org .\nL331: \nL332: Note\nL333: When performing asynchronous tasks in your queue handler that iterates through messages, use an asynchronous version of iterating through your messages. For example, `for (const m of batch.messages)`or `await Promise.all(batch.messages.map(work))` allow for waiting for the results of asynchronous calls. `batch.messages.forEach()` does not.\nL334: ### `MessageBatch`\nL335: \nL336: A batch of messages that are sent to a consumer Worker.\nL337: \nL338:     `interface MessageBatch<Body = unknown> {\nL339:       readonly queue: string;\nL340:       readonly messages: readonly Message<Body>[];\nL341:       ackAll(): void;\nL342:       retryAll(options?: QueueRetryOptions): void;\nL343:     }`\nL344:   * `queue` `string`\nL345:     * The name of the Queue that belongs to this batch.\nL346:   * `messages` `Message[]`\nL347:     * An array of messages in the batch. Ordering of messages is best effort -- not guaranteed to be exactly the same as the order in which they were published.\nL348:   * `ackAll()` `void`\nL349:     * Marks every message as successfully delivered, regardless of whether your `queue()` consumer handler returns successfully or not.\nL350:   * `retryAll(options?: QueueRetryOptions)` `void`\nL351:     * Marks every message to be retried in the next batch.\nL352:     * Supports an optional `options` object.\nL353: ### `Message`\nL354: \nL355: A message that is sent to a consumer Worker.\nL356: \nL357:     `interface Message<Body = unknown> {\nL358:       readonly id: string;\nL359:       readonly timestamp: Date;\nL360:       readonly body: Body;\nL361:     \treadonly attempts: number;\nL362:       ack(): void;\nL363:       retry(options?: QueueRetryOptions): void;\nL364:     }`\nL365:   * `id` `string`\nL366:     * A unique, system-generated ID for the message.\nL367:   * `timestamp` `Date`\nL368:     * A timestamp when the message was sent.\nL369:   * `body` `unknown`\nL370:     * The body of the message.\nL371:     * The body can be any type supported by the cite51†structured clone algorithm ↗†developer.mozilla.org , as long as its size is less than 128 KB.\nL372:   * `attempts` `number`\nL373:     * The number of times the consumer has attempted to process this message. D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:188:`INGEST_MODE` is a case-sensitive exact allowlist with only `normal`, `store_only`, and `reject`. Missing, empty, mixed-case, or otherwise unknown values resolve to `reject`; raw configuration values are never returned or logged. In `normal`, the HTTP producer validates the envelope, persists immutable raw R2 plus a D1 row, and sends `{submission_id}` to the Queue. In `store_only`, it validates and persists raw R2 plus a D1 row that remains `received`, but performs zero Queue sends. In `reject`, including the fail-closed missing/unknown cases, the endpoint accepts nothing and performs no R2, D1, or Queue write.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:190:In `normal`, the producer persists `received`, sends `{submission_id}` to the at-least-once Queue, and confirms the send with `received|queued|retryable -> queued`; the `queued -> queued` case is required for scheduled resend confirmation. Queue delivery may run before that confirmation, so the consumer must compare-transition `received|queued|retryable -> validating`. A successful confirmation refreshes `updated_at` and clears `ingest_paused` or any other stale `safe_error`. An ambiguous Queue failure returns `queued` only when a reread proves consumer progress beyond `queued`; otherwise an eligible `received|queued|retryable` row is confirmed `retryable`.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:192:The downstream path is `validating -> validated | rejected -> staged -> published`; exhausted work becomes `dead_letter`. Retryable failures use bounded exponential backoff. Every consumer action and state transition is idempotent. A failed enqueue attempt increments `retry_count`, refreshes `updated_at`, and records only a safe error; a later successful enqueue clears that stale error. Raw R2 storage occurs before the Worker returns an accepted receipt.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:196:The service target is at least 100 concurrent notebook clients. HTTP handlers do not call GitHub synchronously. In `normal`, they persist to R2/D1 and enqueue work, then return `202` with submission ids; in `store_only`, they persist the accepted receipt without enqueueing, as specified above. Queue consumers scale independently.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:200:A normal concurrent duplicate waits a bounded interval for the first producer and reuses its receipt without another Queue send. If the shared row is still `received` after the final reread, the duplicate resends the same `{submission_id}` and confirms `queued`/later or `retryable`; this recovery may create another Queue delivery, so downstream consumer idempotency is mandatory. All such deliveries still reference one D1 row and one retained winning raw object.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:202:The Worker scheduled handler is a strict no-op unless the resolved mode is `normal`. In `normal` it runs a bounded recovery page over stale `received|queued|retryable` rows, using the `(state,updated_at)` index and a `staleBefore` cutoff as backoff eligibility. It resends the same `{submission_id}`, uses checked state transitions, retains raw R2, and advances failed retry metadata so one hot page cannot starve older tail rows. A successful stale `queued` resend performs `queued -> queued`, refreshes `updated_at`, and clears `ingest_paused` or another stale `safe_error`, so the same row cannot flood every cron page. Thus a `store_only` row remains `received` even after it is stale; switching to `normal` lets the next scheduled run recover that same id once per eligible scan.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:204:Queue backlog delivery resolves `INGEST_MODE` before inspecting the message body. For every non-`normal` mode, including missing/unknown values, it parses only `submission_id` and conditionally parks D1 `received|queued|retryable -> retryable` with `safe_error=ingest_paused`, unchanged `retry_count`, refreshed `updated_at`, and the raw R2 reference untouched. It rereads and verifies that durable park, then ACKs; a successful park never calls `message.retry()` and therefore consumes no Cloudflare `max_retries` attempt. Before that ACK it performs no R2 read, replay, publisher enqueue, token/GitHub access, payload log, or raw-mode log. A duplicate delivery repeats the same idempotent D1 park and still leaves one row. A D1 exception or unverifiable transition is not ACKed and follows the platform retry/exhaustion path; the unchanged stale row remains recoverable. When mode returns to `normal`, scheduled recovery resends the same parked `retryable` id, and it also rescues a stale `queued` row left after park failures exhausted Queue delivery.
D:\100XH100\.worktrees\cayleypy-results-ingest\docs\superpowers\specs\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:206:At-least-once delivery cannot duplicate published results because D1 and repository paths use the deterministic idempotency key/submission id. R2 raw payloads and DLQ make accepted work recoverable even during GitHub, DNS, Queue, or consumer outages.

submission_id: submissionId,\n    idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,\n    state: \"received\",\n    duplicate: false,\n  };\n}\n\r\n### worker.ts\r\nimport { findBySubmissionId } from \"./db.js\";\nimport { MAX_SERIALIZED_BATCH_BYTES, validateBatch, validateBatchIntegrity, type ResultEnvelopeV1 } from \"./schema.js\";\nimport {\n  SafeIngestError,\n  receiveEnvelope,\n  receiveEnvelopeStoreOnly,\n  recoverStaleSubmissions,\n  type IngestEnv,\n} from \"./storage.js\";\n\nexport type IngestMode = \"normal\" | \"store_only\" | \"reject\";\n\nexport interface IngestRateLimit {\n  limit(input: { key: string }): Promise<{ success: boolean }>;\n}\n\nexport interface WorkerEnv extends IngestEnv {\n  INGEST_MODE?: string;\n  INGEST_RATE_LIMIT?: IngestRateLimit;\n}\n\nexport const MAX_REQUEST_BYTES = MAX_SERIALIZED_BATCH_BYTES;\nexport const MAX_RESULTS_PER_REQUEST = 100;\nexport const PER_IP_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE = 30;\nexport const GLOBAL_ENVELOPES_PER_MINUTE = 2_000;\nexport const RECOVERY_STALE_MS = 60_000;\nexport const RECOVERY_LIMIT = 50;\nconst RATE_WINDOW_MS = 60_000;\nconst RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS = 60;\nconst ENVELOPE_CONCURRENCY = 8;\nconst DUPLICATE_REREAD_BUDGET = 400;\n\nclass SafeHttpError extends Error {\n  constructor(readonly status: number, readonly code: string) {\n    super(code);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function resolveIngestMode(value: unknown): IngestMode {\n  if (value === \"normal\" || value === \"store_only\" || value === \"reject\") return value;\n  return \"reject\";\n}\n\nfunction jsonResponse(value: unknown, status = 200, headers: HeadersInit = {}): Response {\n  const responseHeaders = new Headers(headers);\n  responseHeaders.set(\"content-type\", \"application/json; charset=utf-8\");\n  responseHeaders.set(\"cache-control\", \"no-store\");\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(value), { status, headers: responseHeaders });\n}\n\nfunction errorResponse(status: number, code: string, headers: HeadersInit = {}): Response {\n  return jsonResponse({ error: code }, status, headers);\n}\n\nfunction methodNotAllowed(allow: \"GET\" | \"POST\"): Response {\n  return errorResponse(405, \"method_not_allowed\", { Allow: allow });\n}\n\nfunction rateLimited(): Response {\n  return errorResponse(429, \"rate_limited\", { \"Retry-After\": String(RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS) });\n}\n\nfunction mediaType(request: Request): string {\n  return (request.headers.get(\"content-type\") ?? \"\").split(\";\", 1)[0].trim().toLowerCase();\n}\n\nfunction declaredBodyLength(request: Request): number | null {\n  const raw = request.headers.get(\"content-length\");\n  if (raw === null) return null;\n  if (!/^\\d+$/.test(raw)) throw new SafeHttpError(400, \"invalid_content_length\");\n  const length = Number(raw);\n  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(length)) throw new SafeHttpError(413, \"request_too_large\");\n  return length;\n}\n\nasync function readBoundedText(request: Request): Promise<{ text: string; rawByteLength: number }> {\n  const declared = declaredBodyLength(request);\n  if (declared !== null && declared > MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {\n    throw new SafeHttpError(413, \"request_too_large\");\n  }\n\n  if (request.body === null) return { text: \"\", rawByteLength: 0 };\n  const reader = request.body.getReader();\n  const decoder = new TextDecoder(\"utf-8\", { fatal: true });\n  let text = \"\";\n  let total = 0;\n  try {\n    while (true) {\n      let chunk: ReadableStreamReadResult<Uint8Array>;\n      try {\n        chunk = await reader.read();\n      } catch {\n        throw new SafeHttpError(400, \"invalid_body\");\n      }\n      if (chunk.done) break;\n      total += chunk.value.byteLength;\n      if (total > MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {\n        try { await reader.cancel(); } catch { /* Best-effort cancellation after the hard bound. */ }\n        throw new SafeHttpError(413, \"request_too_large\");\n      }\n      try {\n        text += decoder.decode(chunk.value, { stream: true });\n      } catch {\n        try { await reader.cancel(); } catch { /* Best-effort cancellation after invalid UTF-8. */ }\n        throw new SafeHttpError(400, \"invalid_json\");\n      }\n    }\n    try {\n      text += decoder.decode();\n    } catch {\n      throw new SafeHttpError(400, \"invalid_json\");\n    }\n    return { text, rawByteLength: total };\n  } catch (error) {\n    text = \"\";\n    throw error;\n  } finally {\n    reader.releaseLock();\n  }\n}\n\nasync function parseBatch(request: Request): Promise<{ value: ResultEnvelopeV1[]; rawByteLength: number } | Response> {\n  if (mediaType(request) !== \"application/json\") {\n    return errorResponse(415, \"unsupported_media_type\");\n  }\n\n  let body: { text: string; rawByteLength: number };\n  try {\n    body = await readBoundedText(request);\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (error instanceof SafeHttpError) return errorResponse(error.status, error.code);\n    return errorResponse(400, \"invalid_body\");\n  }\n\n  let parsed: unknown;\n  try {\n    parsed = JSON.parse(body.text);\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(400, \"invalid_json\");\n  } finally {\n    body.text = \"\";\n  }\n  const validation = validateBatch(parsed, body.rawByteLength);\n  if (!validation.ok) {\n    return jsonResponse({ error: \"invalid_schema\", errors: validation.errors }, 400);\n  }\n  const integrityErrors = await validateBatchIntegrity(validation.value.results);\n  if (integrityErrors.length !== 0) {\n    return jsonResponse({ error: \"invalid_schema\", errors: integrityErrors }, 400);\n  }\n  return { value: validation.value.results, rawByteLength: body.rawByteLength };\n}\nasync function consumeD1Limit(\n  db: D1Database,\n  scope: string,\n  amount: number,\n  maximum: number,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const windowStart = Math.floor(Date.now() / RATE_WINDOW_MS) * RATE_WINDOW_MS;\n  const result = await db.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO ingest_rate_limits (scope,window_start,count) VALUES (?,?,?)\n     ON CONFLICT(scope) DO UPDATE SET\n       window_start = excluded.window_start,\n       count = CASE\n         WHEN ingest_rate_limits.window_start = excluded.window_start\n         THEN ingest_rate_limits.count + excluded.count\n         ELSE excluded.count\n       END\n     WHERE\n       (excluded.window_start > ingest_rate_limits.window_start AND excluded.count <= ?)\n       OR\n       (excluded.window_start = ingest_rate_limits.window_start\n        AND ingest_rate_limits.count + excluded.count <= ?)`,\n  ).bind(scope, windowStart, amount, maximum, maximum).run();\n  return result.meta.changes === 1;\n}\n\nasync function allowIpRequest(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv): Promise<boolean> {\n  const ip = request.headers.get(\"CF-Connecting-IP\")?.trim() || \"unknown\";\n  if (env.INGEST_RATE_LIMIT !== undefined) {\n    try {\n      if (!(await env.INGEST_RATE_LIMIT.limit({ key: `ip:${ip}` })).success) return false;\n    } catch {\n      // The D1 counter below remains the authoritative fallback.\n    }\n  }\n  return consumeD1Limit(env.RESULTS_DB, `ip:${ip}`, 1, PER_IP_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE);\n}\nfunction receiptResponse(\n  request: Request,\n  receipt: { submission_id: string; idempotency_key: string },\n): { submission_id: string; idempotency_key: string; status_url: string } {\n  return {\n    submission_id: receipt.submission_id,\n    idempotency_key: receipt.idempotency_key,\n    status_url: new URL(`/v1/submissions/${receipt.submission_id}`, request.url).toString(),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction safeIngestCode(error: unknown): string {\n  return error instanceof SafeIngestError ? error.code : \"ingest_failed\";\n}\n\nasync function mapBounded<T, U>(\n  values: readonly T[],\n  concurrency: number,\n  operation: (value: T, index: number) => Promise<U>,\n): Promise<U[]> {\n  const output = new Array<U>(values.length);\n  let nextIndex = 0;\n  const consume = async () => {\n    while (nextIndex < values.length) {\n      const index = nextIndex;\n      nextIndex += 1;\n      output[index] = await operation(values[index], index);\n    }\n  };\n  await Promise.all(Array.from(\n    { length: Math.min(concurrency, values.length) },\n    () => consume(),\n  ));\n  return output;\n}\nasync function handlePostResults(request: Request, env: WorkerEnv): Promise<Response> {\n  const mode = resolveIngestMode(env.INGEST_MODE);\n  if (mode === \"reject\") return errorResponse(503, \"ingest_disabled\");\n\n  let ipAllowed: boolean;\n  try {\n    ipAllowed = await allowIpRequest(request, env);\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(503, \"rate_limit_unavailable\");\n  }\n  if (!ipAllowed) return rateLimited();\n\n  const parsed = await parseBatch(request);\n  if (parsed instanceof Response) return parsed;\n\n  let globallyAllowed: boolean;\n  try {\n    globallyAllowed = await consumeD1Limit(\n      env.RESULTS_DB,\n      \"global\",\n      parsed.value.length,\n      GLOBAL_ENVELOPES_PER_MINUTE,\n    );\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(503, \"rate_limit_unavailable\");\n  }\n  if (!globallyAllowed) return rateLimited();\n\n  const duplicatePollAttempts = Math.max(\n    1,\n    Math.floor(DUPLICATE_REREAD_BUDGET / parsed.value.length),\n  );\n  const outcomes = await mapBounded(parsed.value, ENVELOPE_CONCURRENCY, async (envelope, index) => {\n    try {\n      const receipt = mode === \"normal\"\n        ? await receiveEnvelope(env, envelope, { duplicatePollAttempts })\n        : await receiveEnvelopeStoreOnly(env, envelope);\n      return { ok: true as const, receipt: receiptResponse(request, receipt) };\n    } catch (error) {\n      return { ok: false as const, error: { index, code: safeIngestCode(error) } };\n    }\n  });\n\n  const receipts = outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome.ok).map((outcome) => outcome.receipt);\n  const errors = outcomes.filter((outcome) => !outcome.ok).map((outcome) => outcome.error);\n  return jsonResponse(errors.length === 0 ? { receipts } : { receipts, errors }, 202);\n}\n\nasync function handleStatus(submissionId: string, env: WorkerEnv): Promise<Response> {\n  let row;\n  try {\n    row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(503, \"status_unavailable\");\n  }\n  if (row === null) return errorResponse(404, \"not_found\");\n  return jsonResponse({\n    submission_id: row.submission_id,\n    idempotency_key: row.idempotency_key,\n    state: row.state,\n    safe_error: row.safe_error,\n    retry_count: row.retry_count,\n    updated_at: row.updated_at,\n  });\n}\n\nfunction health(env: WorkerEnv): Response {\n  return jsonResponse({\n    status: \"ok\",\n    ingest_mode: resolveIngestMode(env.INGEST_MODE),\n    limits: {\n      max_request_bytes: MAX_REQUEST_BYTES,\n      max_results_per_request: MAX_RESULTS_PER_REQUEST,\n      per_ip_requests_per_minute: PER_IP_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE,\n      global_envelopes_per_minute: GLOBAL_ENVELOPES_PER_MINUTE,\n    },\n    recovery: { stale_ms: RECOVERY_STALE_MS, limit: RECOVERY_LIMIT },\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function fetchRequest(\n  request: Request,\n  env: WorkerEnv,\n  _ctx: ExecutionContext,\n): Promise<Response> {\n  const pathname = new URL(request.url).pathname;\n  if (pathname === \"/v1/results\") {\n    if (request.method !== \"POST\") return methodNotAllowed(\"POST\");\n    return handlePostResults(request, env);\n  }\n  if (pathname === \"/healthz\") {\n    if (request.method !== \"GET\") return methodNotAllowed(\"GET\");\n    return health(env);\n  }\n  const statusMatch = /^\\/v1\\/submissions\\/([^/]+)$/.exec(pathname);\n  if (statusMatch !== null) {\n    if (request.method !== \"GET\") return methodNotAllowed(\"GET\");\n    let submissionId: string;\n    try {\n      submissionId = decodeURIComponent(statusMatch[1]);\n    } catch {\n      return errorResponse(400, \"invalid_submission_id\");\n    }\n    return handleStatus(submissionId, env);\n  }\n  return errorResponse(404, \"not_found\");\n}\n\nexport async function scheduled(\n  controller: ScheduledController,\n  env: WorkerEnv,\n  _ctx: ExecutionContext,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (resolveIngestMode(env.INGEST_MODE) !== \"normal\") return;\n  await recoverStaleSubmissions(env, {\n    staleBefore: new Date(controller.scheduledTime - RECOVERY_STALE_MS),\n    limit: RECOVERY_LIMIT,\n  });\n}\n\nexport default { fetch: fetchRequest, scheduled } satisfies ExportedHandler<WorkerEnv>;\n\r\n"

id : null;\n}\n\nfunction isTerminal(state: SubmissionRow[\"state\"]): boolean {\n  return state === \"validated\" || state === \"rejected\" || state === \"staged\" || state === \"published\" || state === \"dead_letter\";\n}\n\nasync function retryable(env: ConsumerEnv, id: string, code: string): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, id, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\"], \"retryable\", {\n      safeError: code,\n      incrementRetryCount: true,\n    });\n  } catch {\n    // Platform retry preserves the durable row even if this annotation fails.\n  }\n}\n\nasync function reject(env: ConsumerEnv, id: string, code: string): Promise<void> {\n  const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, id, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\"], \"rejected\", { safeError: code });\n  if (!changed) {\n    const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, id);\n    if (!current || !isTerminal(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n  }\n}\n\nasync function validateRawEnvelope(env: ConsumerEnv, row: SubmissionRow): Promise<\"valid\" | \"invalid\"> {\n  const object = await env.RAW_RESULTS.get(row.raw_r2_key);\n  if (object === null) throw new Error(\"raw_missing\");\n  let candidate: unknown;\n  try {\n    candidate = JSON.parse(await object.text());\n  } catch {\n    return \"invalid\";\n  }\n  let encoded: number;\n  try {\n    encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJson({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] })).byteLength;\n  } catch {\n    return \"invalid\";\n  }\n  const batch = validateBatch({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] }, encoded);\n  if (!batch.ok) return \"invalid\";\n  const envelope = batch.value.results[0] as ResultEnvelopeV1;\n  if (envelope.idempotency_key !== row.idempotency_key) return \"invalid\";\n  return (await validateEnvelopeIntegrity(envelope)).length === 0 ? \"valid\" : \"invalid\";\n}\n\n/**\n * Consumes one at-least-once validation message. Terminal rows ACK idempotently.\n * Paused modes never read R2 or replay a payload.\n */\nexport async function consumeValidationMessage(\n  message: QueueMessageLike,\n  env: ConsumerEnv,\n  mode: ConsumerMode,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const submissionId = queueSubmissionId(message.body);\n  if (submissionId === null) {\n    message.ack();\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (mode !== \"normal\") {\n    try {\n      await parkPausedSubmission(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      message.ack();\n    } catch {\n      message.retry();\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  let row: SubmissionRow | null;\n  try {\n    row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n  } catch {\n    message.retry();\n    return;\n  }\n  if (row === null || isTerminal(row.state)) {\n    message.ack();\n    return;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"retryable\"], \"validating\", { safeError: null });\n    if (!changed) {\n      row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (row === null || isTerminal(row.state)) {\n        message.ack();\n        return;\n      }\n      if (row.state !== \"validating\") throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    }\n    const current = row ?? await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n    if (current === null) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    if (await validateRawEnvelope(env, current) === \"invalid\") {\n      await reject(env, submissionId, \"invalid_envelope\");\n    } else {\n      const validated = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"validating\"], \"validated\", { safeError: null });\n      if (!validated) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    }\n    message.ack();\n  } catch {\n    await retryable(env, submissionId, \"validation_unavailable\");\n    message.retry();\n  }\n}\n'@; $lines=$body.TrimEnd(\"`r\",\"`n\") -split \"`r?`n\"; $patch=@(\"diff --git a/$path b/$path\",\"new file mode 100644\",\"index 0000000..2222222\",\"--- /dev/null\",\"+++ b/$path\",\"@@ -0,0 +1,$($lines.Count) @@\") + ($lines | ForEach-Object { '+' + $_ }); $patch -join \"`n\" | git -C $wt apply --whitespace=nowarn --ignore-space-change; git -C $wt status --short","workdir":"D:\\100XH100","timeout_ms":10000,"login":false}); text(JSON.stringify(r));

D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-results-ingest\\docs\\superpowers\\specs\\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:186:### 9.2 Operating modes and state machine\r\n  D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-results-ingest\\docs\\superpowers\\specs\\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:187:\r\n- GitHub App: repository-scoped write credential stored only as Cloudflare secrets.\r\n- GitHub Actions: independent schema/path replay validation and staging-to-main merge gate.\r\n\r\n### 9.2 Operating modes and state machine\r\n\r\n  D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-results-ingest\\docs\\superpowers\\specs\\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:188:`INGEST_MODE` is a case-sensitive exact allowlist with only `normal`, `store_only`, and `reject`. Missing, empty, mixed-case, or otherwise unknown values resolve to `reject`; raw configuration values are never returned or logged. In `normal`, the HTTP producer validates the envelope, persists immutable raw R2 plus a D1 row, and sends `{submission_id}` to the Queue. In `store_only`, it validates and persists raw R2 plus a D1 row that remains `received`, but performs zero Queue sends. In `reject`, including the fail-closed missing/unknown cases, the endpoint accepts nothing and performs no R2, D1, or Queue write.\r\n  D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-results-ingest\\docs\\superpowers\\specs\\2026-07-28-public-cayleypy-2xt4-results-design.md:189:\r\n> D:

\"./schema.js\";\nimport { parkPausedSubmission, SafeIngestError, type IngestEnv } from \"./storage.js\";\n\nexport type ConsumerMode = \"normal\" | \"store_only\" | \"reject\";\nexport const MAX_QUEUE_ATTEMPTS = 8;\nexport const MAX_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS = 300;\n\nexport interface QueueMessageLike {\n  body: unknown;\n  attempts?: number;\n  ack(): void;\n  retry(options?: { delaySeconds?: number }): void;\n}\n\nexport interface GitHubWriterNamespace {\n  getByName(name: string): { enqueueValidated(submissionId: string): Promise<void> };\n}\n\nexport interface ConsumerEnv extends IngestEnv {\n  INGEST_MODE?: string;\n  GITHUB_WRITER?: GitHubWriterNamespace;\n}\n\nexport function queueSubmissionId(body: unknown): string | null {\n  if (body === null || typeof body !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(body)) return null;\n  const id = (body as Record<string, unknown>).submission_id;\n  return typeof id === \"string\" && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/.test(id) ? id : null;\n}\n\nexport function retryDelaySeconds(retryCount: number): number {\n  const exponent = Math.max(0, Math.min(8, Math.trunc(retryCount)));\n  return Math.min(MAX_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS, 2 ** exponent);\n}\n\nfunction isTerminal(state: SubmissionRow[\"state\"]): boolean {\n  return state === \"rejected\" || state === \"staged\" || state === \"published\" || state === \"dead_letter\";\n}\n\nfunction exhausted(message: QueueMessageLike): boolean {\n  return (message.attempts ?? 1) >= MAX_QUEUE_ATTEMPTS;\n}\n\nasync function enqueueValidated(env: ConsumerEnv, submissionId: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (env.GITHUB_WRITER === undefined) throw new Error(\"github_writer_unavailable\");\n  await env.GITHUB_WRITER.getByName(\"cayleypy-results-v1\").enqueueValidated(submissionId);\n}\n\nasync function retryable(env: ConsumerEnv, id: string, code: string, attempts: number): Promise<number> {\n  try {\n    const to = attempts >= MAX_QUEUE_ATTEMPTS ? \"dead_letter\" : \"retryable\";\n    const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, id, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\", \"validated\"], to, {\n      safeError: code,\n      incrementRetryCount: true,\n    });\n    if (!changed) {\n      const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, id);\n      if (!current || !isTerminal(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n      return current.retry_count;\n    }\n    const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, id);\n    if (!current) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    return current.retry_count;\n  } catch {\n    // Platform retry retains the original durable row when annotation itself fails.\n    return 0;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function reject(env: ConsumerEnv, id: string, code: string): Promise<void> {\n  const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, id, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\"], \"rejected\", { safeError: code });\n  if (!changed) {\n    const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, id);\n    if (!current || !isTerminal(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n  }\n}\n\nasync function validateRawEnvelope(env: ConsumerEnv, row: SubmissionRow): Promise<\"valid\" | \"invalid\"> {\n  const object = await env.RAW_RESULTS.get(row.raw_r2_key);\n  if (object === null) throw new Error(\"raw_missing\");\n  let candidate: unknown;\n  try { candidate = JSON.parse(await object.text()); } catch { return \"invalid\"; }\n  let encoded: number;\n  try { encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJson({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] })).byteLength; } catch { return \"invalid\"; }\n  const batch = validateBatch({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] }, encoded);\n  if (!batch.ok) return \"invalid\";\n  const envelope = batch.value.results[0] as ResultEnvelopeV1;\n  if (envelope.idempotency_key !== row.idempotency_key) return \"invalid\";\n  return (await validateEnvelopeIntegrity(envelope)).length === 0 ? \"valid\" : \"invalid\";\n}\n\nasync function retryMessage(message: QueueMessageLike, env: ConsumerEnv, submissionId: string, code: string): Promise<void> {\n  const attempts = message.attempts ?? 1;\n  const retryCount = await retryable(env, submissionId, code, attempts);\n  message.retry(exhausted(message) ? undefined : { delaySeconds: retryDelaySeconds(retryCount) });\n}\n\nasync function retryValidatedPublisher(message: QueueMessageLike, env: ConsumerEnv, submissionId: string): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await enqueueValidated(env, submissionId);\n    const refreshed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"validated\"], \"validated\", { safeError: null });\n    if (!refreshed) {\n      const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (!current || !isTerminal(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    }\n    message.ack();\n  } catch {\n    // Keep validated until exhaustion; a duplicate delivery re-enqueues idempotently.\n    await retryMessage(message, env, submissionId, \"publisher_unavailable\");\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Consumes one at-least-once validation message. Paused modes resolve before\n * payload inspection and never read R2 or call the publisher.\n */\nexport async function consumeValidationMessage(\n  message: QueueMessageLike,\n  env: ConsumerEnv,\n  mode: ConsumerMode,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (mode !== \"normal\") {\n    const submissionId = queueSubmissionId(message.body);\n    if (submissionId === null) { message.ack(); return; }\n    try {\n      const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (current === null || isTerminal(current.state) || current.state === \"validated\") {\n        message.ack();\n        return;\n      }\n      await parkPausedSubmission(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      message.ack();\n    } catch { message.retry(); }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const submissionId = queueSubmissionId(message.body);\n  if (submissionId === null) { message.ack(); return; }\n  let row: SubmissionRow | null;\n  try { row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId); } catch { message.retry(); return; }\n  if (row === null || isTerminal(row.state)) { message.ack(); return; }\n  if (row.state === \"validated\") {\n    await retryValidatedPublisher(message, env, submissionId);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"retryable\"], \"validating\", { safeError: null });\n    if (!changed) {\n      row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (row === null || isTerminal(row.state)) { message.ack(); return; }\n      if (row.state === \"validated\") { await retryValidatedPublisher(message, env, submissionId); return; }\n      if (row.state !== \"validating\") throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    }\n    const current = row ?? await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n    if (current === null) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    if (await validateRawEnvelope(env, current) === \"invalid\") {\n      await reject(env, submissionId, \"invalid_envelope\");\n      message.ack();\n      return;\n    }\n    const validated = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"validating\"], \"validated\", { safeError: null });\n    if (!validated) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    await retryValidatedPublisher(message, env, submissionId);\n  } catch {\n    await retryMessage(message, env, submissionId, \"validation_unavailable\");\n  }\n}\n'@; $tmp='_patchtmp_consumer.ts'; [IO.File]::WriteAllText(\"$wt\\$tmp\",$body.TrimStart(\"`r\",\"`n\"),(New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false))); $diff=git -C $wt diff --no-index --relative -- $rel $tmp; $diff=$diff -replace \"b/$tmp\",\"b/$rel\"; $diff | git -C $wt apply --whitespace=nowarn --ignore-space-change; Remove-Item -LiteralPath \"$wt\\$tmp\"; git -C $wt diff --check","workdir":"D:\\100XH100","timeout_ms":10000,"login":false}); text(JSON.stringify(r));

retried: number; retryOptions: Array<{ delaySeconds?: number } | undefined> };\nfunction message(submissionId: string, attempts = 1): ProbeMessage {\n  return {\n    body: { submission_id: submissionId }, attempts, acked: 0, retried: 0, retryOptions: [],\n    ack() { this.acked += 1; },\n    retry(options) { this.retried += 1; this.retryOptions.push(options); },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction bindings(\n  raw: R2Bucket = env.RAW_RESULTS,\n  enqueue: (id: string) => Promise<void> = async () => undefined,\n): ConsumerEnv {\n  return {\n    RESULTS_DB: env.RESULTS_DB,\n    RAW_RESULTS: raw,\n    VALIDATE_QUEUE: { send: async () => undefined } as unknown as Queue,\n    GITHUB_WRITER: { getByName: () => ({ enqueueValidated: enqueue }) },\n  };\n}\n\nasync function received(inputEnv: ConsumerEnv = bindings()): Promise<{ id: string; env: ConsumerEnv }> {\n  const input = envelope();\n  input.idempotency_key = await computeIdempotency(input);\n  const receipt = await receiveEnvelope(inputEnv as IngestEnv, input);\n  return { id: receipt.submission_id, env: inputEnv };\n}\n\nasync function state(id: string): Promise<{ state: string; safe_error: string | null; retry_count: number }> {\n  return (await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"SELECT state, safe_error, retry_count FROM submissions WHERE submission_id = ?\")\n    .bind(id).first<{ state: string; safe_error: string | null; retry_count: number }>())!;\n}\n\nbeforeEach(async () => {\n  await env.RESULTS_DB.exec(\"DELETE FROM submissions\");\n  await env.RESULTS_DB.exec(\"DELETE FROM ingest_rate_limits\");\n  const listed = await env.RAW_RESULTS.list({ prefix: \"raw/\" });\n  if (listed.objects.length) await env.RAW_RESULTS.delete(listed.objects.map((object) => object.key));\n});\n\ndescribe(\"Task 4 Queue replay consumer\", () => {\n  test(\"replays an immutable raw envelope and ACKs after writer enqueue\", async () => {\n    const seeded = await received();\n    const queued = message(seeded.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(queued, seeded.env, \"normal\");\n    expect(queued).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n    expect(await state(seeded.id)).toEqual({ state: \"validated\", safe_error: null, retry_count: 0 });\n  });\n\n  test(\"rejects immutable malformed raw without a retry\", async () => {\n    const seeded = await received();\n    const row = await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"SELECT raw_r2_key FROM submissions WHERE submission_id = ?\").bind(seeded.id).first<{ raw_r2_key: string }>();\n    await env.RAW_RESULTS.put(row!.raw_r2_key, canonicalJson({ not: \"an envelope\" }));\n    const queued = message(seeded.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(queued, seeded.env, \"normal\");\n    expect(queued).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n    expect(await state(seeded.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"rejected\", safe_error: \"invalid_envelope\" });\n  });\n\n  test(\"all non-normal modes park recoverable rows before R2 or writer access\", async () => {\n    for (const mode of [\"store_only\", \"reject\"] as const) {\n      const persisted = await received();\n      const forbidden = bindings({ get: async () => { throw new Error(\"R2 must not be read while paused\"); } } as unknown as R2Bucket,\n        async () => { throw new Error(\"writer must not be called while paused\"); });\n      const queued = message(persisted.id);\n      await consumeValidationMessage(queued, forbidden, mode);\n      expect(queued).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n      expect(await state(persisted.id)).toEqual({ state: \"retryable\", safe_error: \"ingest_paused\", retry_count: 0 });\n    }\n  });\n\n  test(\"malformed and already-terminal deliveries ACK without touching downstream bindings\", async () => {\n    const malformed: ProbeMessage = {\n      body: { submission_id: \"not-a-uuid\" }, attempts: 1, acked: 0, retried: 0, retryOptions: [],\n      ack() { this.acked += 1; }, retry(options) { this.retried += 1; this.retryOptions.push(options); },\n    };\n    await consumeValidationMessage(malformed, bindings(), \"normal\");\n    expect(malformed).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n\n    const seeded = await received();\n    await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"UPDATE submissions SET state = 'rejected' WHERE submission_id = ?\").bind(seeded.id).run();\n    const terminal = message(seeded.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(terminal, bindings({ get: async () => { throw new Error(\"forbidden\"); } } as unknown as R2Bucket), \"normal\");\n    expect(terminal).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n  });\n\n  test(\"enforces 120 logical state bytes and unknown move replay deterministically\", async () => {\n    const oversized = envelope();\n    oversized.model.manifest.state_len = 121;\n    expect(await validateEnvelopeIntegrity(oversized)).toContainEqual({ path: \"/model/manifest/state_len\", keyword: \"stateLength\" });\n    expect(replayPath([0, 1], [\"missing\"], { a: [0, 1] }, 2)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: \"unknown_move\" });\n    expect(replayPath([0], [], {}, 121)).toEqual({ ok: false, code: \"state_length\" });\n  });\n\n  test(\"transient R2 failures become retryable with bounded backoff, then validate\", async () => {\n    const persisted = await received();\n    let reads = 0;\n    const flakyRaw = {\n      get: async (key: string) => {\n        reads += 1;\n        if (reads === 1) throw new Error(\"temporary R2 failure\");\n        return env.RAW_RESULTS.get(key);\n      },\n    } as unknown as R2Bucket;\n    const first = message(persisted.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(first, bindings(flakyRaw), \"normal\");\n    expect(first).toMatchObject({ acked: 0, retried: 1 });\n    expect(first.retryOptions).toEqual([{ delaySeconds: retryDelaySeconds(1) }]);\n    expect(await state(persisted.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"retryable\", safe_error: \"validation_unavailable\", retry_count: 1 });\n\n    const second = message(persisted.id, 2);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(second, bindings(), \"normal\");\n    expect(second).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n    expect(await state(persisted.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"validated\", safe_error: null });\n  });\n\n  test(\"transient D1 lookup retries without acknowledging or reading R2\", async () => {\n    const persisted = await received();\n    const transientDb = { prepare: () => { throw new Error(\"temporary D1 failure\"); } } as unknown as D1Database;\n    const queued = message(persisted.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(queued, { ...bindings(), RESULTS_DB: transientDb }, \"normal\");\n    expect(queued).toMatchObject({ acked: 0, retried: 1 });\n    expect(await state(persisted.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"queued\", retry_count: 0 });\n  });\n\n  test(\"exhaustion records dead_letter and delegates the final delivery to the configured DLQ\", async () => {\n    const persisted = await received();\n    const unavailable = bindings({ get: async () => { throw new Error(\"R2 unavailable\"); } } as unknown as R2Bucket);\n    const queued = message(persisted.id, MAX_QUEUE_ATTEMPTS);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(queued, unavailable, \"normal\");\n    expect(queued).toMatchObject({ acked: 0, retried: 1 });\n    expect(queued.retryOptions).toEqual([undefined]);\n    expect(await state(persisted.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"dead_letter\", safe_error: \"validation_unavailable\", retry_count: 1 });\n  });\n\n  test(\"duplicate validated delivery repeats idempotent writer enqueue and clears stale safe_error\", async () => {\n    let calls = 0;\n    const flaky = bindings(env.RAW_RESULTS, async () => {\n      calls += 1;\n      if (calls === 1) throw new Error(\"writer temporary\");\n    });\n    const seeded = await received(flaky);\n    const first = message(seeded.id);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(first, flaky, \"normal\");\n    expect(await state(seeded.id)).toMatchObject({ state: \"validated\", safe_error: \"publisher_unavailable\", retry_count: 1 });\n\n    const second = message(seeded.id, 2);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(second, flaky, \"normal\");\n    expect(second).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n    expect(await state(seeded.id)).toEqual({ state: \"validated\", safe_error: null, retry_count: 1 });\n\n    const duplicate = message(seeded.id, 3);\n    await consumeValidationMessage(duplicate, flaky, \"normal\");\n    expect(duplicate).toMatchObject({ acked: 1, retried: 0 });\n    expect(calls).toBe(3);\n  });\n\n  test(\"scheduled recovery preserves validating, validated, and dead_letter states\", async () => {\n    const a = await received();\n    const b = await received();\n    const c = await received();\n    await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"UPDATE submissions SET state = 'validating', updated_at = ? WHERE submission_id = ?\").bind(\"2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z\", a.id).run();\n    await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"UPDATE submissions SET state = 'validated', updated_at = ? WHERE submission_id = ?\").bind(\"2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z\", b.id).run();\n    await env.RESULTS_DB.prepare(\"UPDATE submissions SET state = 'dead_letter', updated_at = ? WHERE submission_id = ?\").bind(\"2000-01-01T00:00:00.000Z\", c.id).run();\n    let sends = 0;\n    const workerEnv: WorkerEnv = { ...bindings(), VALIDATE_QUEUE: { send: async () => { sends += 1; } } as unknown as Queue, INGEST_MODE: \"normal\" };\n    await scheduled({ scheduledTime: Date.now() } as ScheduledController, workerEnv, {} as ExecutionContext);\n    expect(sends).toBe(0);\n    expect((await state(a.id)).state).toBe(\"validating\");\n    expect((await state(b.id)).state).toBe(\"validated\");\n    expect((await state(c.id)).state).toBe(\"dead_letter\");\n  });\n});\n'@; $tmp='_patchtmp_consumer_test.ts'; [IO.File]::WriteAllText(\"$wt\\$tmp\",$body.TrimStart(\"`r\",\"`n\"),(New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false))); $diff=git -C $wt diff --no-index --relative -- $rel $tmp; $diff=$diff -replace \"b/$tmp\",\"b/$rel\"; $diff | git -C $wt apply --whitespace=nowarn --ignore-space-change; Remove-Item -LiteralPath \"$wt\\$tmp\"; git -C $wt diff --check","workdir":"D:\\100XH100","timeout_ms":10000,"login":false}); text(JSON.stringify(r));

const r = await tools.shell_command({"command":"$wt='D:\\100XH100\\.worktrees\\cayleypy-results-ingest'; $path='test_results/cayleypy_results_ingest_task4_static_audit_2026-07-29.md'; $body=@'\n# CayleyPy Results Ingest Task 4 static audit — 2026-07-29\n\nScope: uncommitted Queue consumer/replay diff only. No CUDA or beam-search source was inspected or changed.\n\n## Contracts checked\n\n- State replay uses pull permutations and rejects `state_len < 1` or `> 120`; padding bytes are not represented in the proof.\n- The Worker resolves `INGEST_MODE` before iterating Queue messages. In non-normal modes the consumer parses only a UUIDv7 `submission_id`, reads only D1, parks recoverable rows to `retryable/ingest_paused`, then ACKs. It performs no R2 replay or writer call.\n- Normal delivery compare-transitions `received|queued|retryable -> validating`; raw R2 is parsed and schema/proof/replay/idempotency checks are repeated server-side.\n- Invalid immutable raw becomes `rejected/invalid_envelope` and ACKs. Transient D1/R2 failure becomes retryable with bounded exponential delay.\n- Attempts at the configured limit transition to `dead_letter` and call `message.retry()` without a delay so Cloudflare moves the retained message to the configured DLQ.\n- `validated` is intentionally not terminal for normal Queue processing. A delivery calls the deterministic GitHub Writer DO. If enqueue or its D1 confirmation is ambiguous, the row remains `validated` (or reaches `dead_letter` only at exhaustion), the safe error is recorded, and the Queue retry will repeat the same idempotent enqueue.\n- A successful writer enqueue self-transitions `validated -> validated` to clear stale safe error. Final repository states ACK without touching R2/writer.\n- Task 3 scheduled recovery remains scoped to `received|queued|retryable`; Task 4 test coverage explicitly preserves `validating`, `validated`, and `dead_letter`.\n\n## Deterministic test additions\n\n`test/consumer.test.ts` covers valid replay, malformed immutable raw, both non-normal modes, poison/terminal deliveries, state length 121, unknown moves, transient R2 and D1, bounded retry delay, retry exhaustion/DLQ state, idempotent duplicate writer delivery, and scheduled-state preservation.\n\n## Verification status\n\n- `git diff --check`: clean except existing CRLF normalization warnings.\n- Local npm is unavailable: Node `v22.22.0` / npm `10.9.4` exits `Exit handler never called!` before creating `node_modules/.bin/tsc`. ## 358. Codex

Time: `2026-07-29T15:28:08.950Z` · source line `3906`

```text
Нашёл сценарий из review: при отказе D1 на маркировке retry строка может остаться `validating`, а старый recovery её не подбирает. Расширяю ровно durable-контракт: stale `validating` переотправляется тем же id, а paused-mode безопасно паркует его в `retryable`; добавлю гонки/режимы в тесты.
```

ConsumerEnv, submissionId: string, code: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (exhausted(message)) {\n    try {\n      const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\", \"validated\"], \"dead_letter\", {\n        safeError: code, incrementRetryCount: true,\n      });\n      if (!changed) {\n        const current = await Promise<void> {\n  const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, id, [\"received\", \"queued\", \"validating\", \"retryable\"], \"rejected\", { safeError: code });\n  if (!changed) {\n    const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, id);\n    if (!current || !isFinalRepositoryState(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n  }\n}\n\nasync function validateRawEnvelope(env: ConsumerEnv, row: SubmissionRow): Promise<\"valid\" | \"invalid\"> {\n  const object = await env.RAW_RESULTS.get(row.raw_r2_key);\n  if (object === null) throw new Error(\"raw_missing\");\n  let candidate: unknown;\n  try { candidate = JSON.parse(await object.text()); } catch { return \"invalid\"; }\n  let encoded: number;\n  try { encoded = new TextEncoder().encode(canonicalJson({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] })).byteLength; } catch { return \"invalid\"; }\n  const batch = validateBatch({ schema_version: 1, results: [candidate] }, encoded);\n  if (!batch.ok) return \"invalid\";\n  const envelope = batch.value.results[0] as ResultEnvelopeV1;\n  if (envelope.idempotency_key !== row.idempotency_key) return \"invalid\";\n  return (await validateEnvelopeIntegrity(envelope)).length === 0 ? \"valid\" : \"invalid\";\n}\n\nasync function enqueueValidatedOrRetry(message: QueueMessageLike, env: ConsumerEnv, submissionId: string): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await enqueueValidated(env, submissionId);\n    const refreshed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"validated\"], \"validated\", { safeError: null });\n    if (!refreshed) {\n      const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (!current || !isFinalRepositoryState(current.state)) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n    }\n    message.ack();\n  } catch {\n    // Keep the row validated for all non-exhausted retries; never regress it.\n    if (exhausted(message)) {\n      await retryOrDeadLetter(message, env, submissionId, \"publisher_unavailable\");\n      return;\n    }\n    try {\n      const changed = await transition(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId, [\"validated\"], \"validated\", {\n        safeError: \"publisher_unavailable\", incrementRetryCount: true,\n      });\n      if (!changed) throw new SafeIngestError(\"state_transition_conflict\");\n delaySeconds: retryDelaySeconds(0) }); }\n  }\n}\n\n/** Queue handler for at-least-once validation. Non-normal modes never read R2. */\nexport async function consumeValidationMessage(message: QueueMessageLike, env: ConsumerEnv, mode: ConsumerMode): Promise<void> {\n  if (mode !== \"normal\") {\n    const submissionId = queueSubmissionId(message.body);\n    if (submissionId === null) { message.ack(); return; }\n    try {\n      const current = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      if (current === null || current.state === \"validating\" || current.state === \"validated\" || current.state === \"dead_letter\" || isFinalRepositoryState(current.state)) {\n        message.ack();\n        return;\n      }\n      await parkPausedSubmission(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId);\n      message.ack();\n    } catch { message.retry({ delaySeconds: retryDelaySeconds(0) }); }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const submissionId = queueSubmissionId(message.body);\n  if (submissionId === null) { message.ack(); return; }\n  let row: SubmissionRow | null;\n  try { row = await findBySubmissionId(env.RESULTS_DB, submissionId); ## User preferences

- when optimizing, the user asked to first РІР‚Сљjust read what has already been doneРІР‚Сњ -> separate validated wins 
from experiments before editing [Task 1]
- preserve the MLP and unrelated Stream paths; use fail-closed explicit backend/policy selection rather than silent fal
lback [Task 1]

- when asking for status, the user said «Коротко опиши что и в каком состоянии сейчас и где остановилось» -> give a con
cise factual status with the explicit stopping point. [Task 1]
- project reports were conducted in Russian -> write user-facing reports in Russian for this project. [Task 1]

## Reusable knowledge

- A project-status audit must check `AGENTS.md`, `git status`, branches/worktrees, `memory/CHANGELOG.md`, `memory/PROMP
TS.md`, and `test_results/`; `git branch` alone is insufficient. At the audit point `main` was `33acff6` and the transf
ormer worktree was `a7a9ada`; do not mix their state. IHES MLP preparation for puzzle IDs 1001/1002 was complete, but S
LURM was blocked by the SSH-key passphrase; transformer still needed a target-hardware end-to-end run. [Task 1]
- On local RTX 3070, runtime SM80 dispatch plus fused rounded bias+LayerNorm preserved the score digest and reached abo
ut `955065.5 candidates/s`; multifamily `m128n128` policies produced about 19.4% paired Stream1→2→3 improvement with ex
act score dumps. `q64k64` beat `q32k64` locally (`8.2158 ms` vs `8.4259 ms`). [Task 2]
- Native solver is real multi-GPU via `torch.distributed.run --nproc-per-node=2`/NCCL. The original Vlad fast path with
 `gpu_ids=0,1` failed because cached tensors stayed on `cuda:0`; its comparable baseline was changed only to one T4 (`G
PU_IDS='0'`). [Task 2][Task 3]

- Decomposed per-head QK/softmax/PV attention loses to launch overhead and global-memory round trips; strided-QKV CUTLA
SS FMHA materially improved T4 performance. LibTorch eager was faster on repeated Kaggle T4 tests, while native CUTLASS
 was stronger locally; validate per hardware. Exact p900 shape: `seq_len=51`, `d_model=256`, `nhead=8`, `head_dim=32`, 
`ff_dim=1024`, `output_dim=24`. [Task 1]
- On local RTX 3070, `m128n128` was selected after 240 exact dumps; full Stream1РІвЂ вЂ™2РІвЂ вЂ™3 improved `103.27 -> 
83.23 ms` (19.4%), with Python `19/19` and production/CUDA CTest `16/16` passing. `q64k64` beat `q32k64` (`8.2158` vs `
8.4259 ms`) with 40 byte-identical dumps. [Task 1]

## Failures and how to do differently

- symptom: a policy is faster locally. cause: SM86/local results were treated as transferable or standalone kernel time
 as production gain. fix: repeat benchmark and byte-exact gate on target hardware, then validate paired Stream1→2→3 or 
the full pipeline. CUDA Graph was slower than eager in repeated Kaggle T4 LibTorch tests, so it is not a universal win.
 [Task 2]
- symptom: p990 result cannot be compared. cause: Kaggle access failed (`127.0.0.1:3067` unavailable, then `403` / `Per
mission 'kernels.get' was denied` / `Error: Forbidden`); `RUNNING` is not a runtime result. fix: restore account access
, download output and rank logs, then compare wall time/progress with GPU count explicit. No run was stopped or modifie
d during the diagnosis; v2 remains unverified. [Task 3]

- symptom: NCU cannot profile. cause: PATH selects NCU `2026.1.1`, incompatible with driver `572.70`. fix: invoke `/opt
/nvidia/nsight-compute/2025.1.1/ncu` explicitly. [Task 1]
- symptom: scalar Q=1 `warp` CLS compiles but differs from CUTLASS. cause: FP32 scalar reduction order differs from ten
sor-core accumulation. fix: reject before benchmarking; accept the tensor-core `q32k64` CLS candidate only after score-
dump equality and 20 timing repetitions in isolated Stream1 and Stream1РІвЂ вЂ™2РІвЂ вЂ™3. [Task 1]

pu-dev-cutlass-nsight:2026-05-24`, mounts `D:/MultiGPUTrainMLP` at `/work`, enables GPUs, and runs the worker [Task 1]

 seconds [Task 1]

ct `$LASTEXITCODE` rather than masking errors with PowerShell pipes [Task 1]

tecode output [Task 1]

ad [Task 2]

manifest matches the evaluator's expectations [Task 1]

eys included `interface.websiteURL`, `privacyPolicyURL`, and `termsOfServiceURL` [Task 1]

ht-rl-20260619` [Task 2]

ismatch. fix: inspect the evaluator's own expected plugin manifest and align fields before retrying [Task 1]
- symptom: 128-game SLURM array dies everywhere. cause: memory pressure, not code logic. fix: lower concurrency and rai
se memory explicitly before rerunning the same H2H shape [Task 2]
- symptom: SCP path hangs or breaks. cause: running `scp` inside the remote shell or targeting fragile Windows paths wi
th a Cyrillic profile. fix: run transfers from the correct local context or skip local download entirely in favor of di
rect HF upload [Task 2]
- symptom: `hf repos create` gets `403 Forbidden`. cause: placeholder namespace. fix: call `hf auth whoami` first and u
se the real namespace [Task 2]

## User preferences

- in this repo, the user wanted implementation grounded in repo/runtime evidence, not high-level design talk alone [Tas
k 1]
- when asked to submit the newest model, the user expected the agent to determine the freshest checkpoint itself [Task 
2]
- when the user asked to hide sensitive GitHub-visible naming, they still preferred a normal commit/push and explicitly
 did not want history cleaning by default [Task 2]

## Reusable knowledge

- if Python sources are missing, `__pycache__/*.pyc` plus `pytest_cache/v/cache/nodeids` can reconstruct a surprising a
mount of API and test intent [Task 1]
- the validated orbit fix was `phase_step = max(0, step - 1)` / `saturating_sub(1)` across Rust/CUDA/Python, and Docker
 was the reliable verification path when local `cargo` was unavailable [Task 1]
- the freshest checkpoint at the time of the rollout was `molab_checkpoints/v8-gpu-resident-ppo-baseline-v3/generation-
0092.bin`, and the submission trio is always `main.py`, `model.bin`, `liborbit_wars_agent.so` under `kaggle_submission/
` [Task 2]
- a real Python Kaggle opponent requires a separate Kaggle-env external training path instead of trying to force it thr
ough the native GPU-resident loop [Task 2]

## Failures and how to do differently

- symptom: `cargo check` fails locally. cause: local PowerShell environment lacked `cargo`. fix: switch to the Docker C
UDA image for build verification [Task 1]
- symptom: training design stalls around Python Kaggle opponents. cause: wrong abstraction; trying to shoehorn them int
o the native loop. fix: add a dedicated Kaggle-env external training path [Task 2]
- symptom: privacy-oriented naming cleanup starts drifting into history rewriting. cause: overreach. fix: rename visibl
e code/strings in the current branch and push normally unless the user explicitly asks for history surgery [Task 2]
